Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The bosons operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 795. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 255 with 1 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_795(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 795. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^255(S, F) is trivial. QED.
